- **cross-review**: 指定された GitHub プルリクエストを、Claude と Codex の 2 モデルでクロスレビューし、 指摘を修正して再レビューする。1 ラウンド目は全重要度、2 ラウンド目以降は前ラウンドの 指摘の解消と新しい blocker だけを対象に、両モデルが揃って収束するまで上限 3 ラウンドで 繰り返してコミット・push する。 同じレビュー観点（docs/review-perspectives/）を両モデルに渡し、両方が指摘した箇所は 確度が高いものとして優先して対応する。Claude・Codex のどちらかが使えない（未インストール・ 未認証・呼び出し失敗）場合は、その旨をユーザーに伝えたうえで使える側だけの自己レビューに 縮退して処理を続ける（停止しない）。引数で PR 番号/URL を受け取り、無ければ対象 PR を ユーザーに確認する。ユーザーが「この PR をクロスレビューして」「claude と codex の両方で レビューして」「二重レビューで直して」「別モデルにもレビューさせて」などと言ったとき、または /za:cross-review を実行したときに使う。za:review の 2 モデル版なので、レビューの見落としを 減らしたい話が出たら積極的に使う。 ([pkshimizu/za](https://github.com/pkshimizu/za/tree/HEAD/skills/cross-review))

For an independent second opinion on an existing result or release claim, use cross-runtime-second-opinion. Verify returned artifacts before relying on completion claims. ([modootoday/marketplace](https://github.com/modootoday/marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/runtime-bridge/skills/cross-runtime-delegation))
- **cross-runtime-exchange**: This skill enables content-addressed interoperability between three Syrup implementations: ([plurigrid/asi](https://github.com/plurigrid/asi/tree/HEAD/skills/cross-runtime-exchange) · ★72)
- **cross-runtime-exchange**: This skill enables content-addressed interoperability between three Syrup implementations: - **Clojure (Babashka)** - `syrup.clj` - **Rust** - `ocapn-syrup` crate - **Zig** - `zig-syrup` ([plurigrid/zig-syrup](https://github.com/plurigrid/zig-syrup/tree/HEAD/skills/cross-runtime-exchange) · ★4)
- **cross-runtime-memory-distiller**: Hermes / OpenClaw 共用的跨宿主记忆蒸馏技能。 从 Claude / Gemini / OpenClaw / Hermes 多源会话中提取归一化事件， 经清洗、打分、分类后写入热记忆（USER.md / MEMORY.md）， 同时产出蒸馏报告、控制面桥接记录和技能候选草稿。 ([XX-Trader/openclaw-hardflow-backup-20260302](https://github.com/XX-Trader/openclaw-hardflow-backup-20260302/tree/HEAD/skills/library/cross-runtime-memory-distiller))
- **cross-runtime-packaging**: Design or review a library, CLI, or package that must run across multiple JavaScript or language runtimes. Use when packaging for Node, browsers, edge runtimes, mobile, or mixed CommonJS and ESM consumers. ([helx0071/skillsmo](https://github.com/helx0071/skillsmo/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/cross-runtime-packaging))
- **cross-runtime-second-opinion**: Obtain or prepare an independent, adversarial second opinion from another agent runtime before trusting a result or approving a release. Use for peer review, requests for another CLI to challenge an existing result, or deciding whether model agreement is sufficient evidence, including reviews whose execution is deferred. Provide a runnable file handoff and a separate local acceptance probe. General work offloading belongs to cross-runtime-delegation. ([modootoday/marketplace](https://github.com/modootoday/marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/runtime-bridge/skills/cross-runtime-second-opinion))
- **cross-runtime-skill-converter**: Convert a Claude Code skill to Codex format or vice versa. Trigger on: "convert my skill to Codex", "port this skill to Claude Code", "make this skill work in both runtimes", "cross-runtime skill", "skill doesn't work in [provider]", "convert [skill-name] to Codex", "bring this Codex skill into Claude Code", "polyskill". Handles structural differences in tool declarations, description truncation, sidecar YAML generation, and dynamic injection syntax translation. ([m2ai-portfolio/m2ai-skills-pack](https://github.com/m2ai-portfolio/m2ai-skills-pack/tree/HEAD/m2ai-model-economics/skills/cross-runtime-skill-converter) · ★4)
- **cross-runtime-validation**: Cross-runtime epistemic validation protocol. Run the same task against multiple independent AI runtimes (different model families, not just different instances) with epistemic blinding, then measure whether convergence predicts correctness. Uses Cohen's kappa for error correlation, verdict accuracy against ground truth, and systematic bias profiling. Produces a capability matrix, error correlation matrix, and convergence analysis. Requires a scored corpus and 2+ runtimes from distinct model families. ([hummbl-io/agent-skills](https://github.com/hummbl-io/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/cross-runtime-validation))
- **cross-sample-aiso-consistency-correlation-analysis**: Use when you have RNA-seq (short or long reads) for at least two comparable samples of the same species or orthologous tissues and need to determine whether each exon's AISO class (upfi vs dofi) and Fupfi value is stable across conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/cross-sample-aiso-consistency-correlation-analysis))
- **cross-sample-alignment-matrix-construction**: Use when you have already generated per-sample MS2 fingerprints (count vectors of MS2 peaks and neutral losses to the precursor) from matchms-processed spectra using spec2vec document representations, and you need to align these fingerprints into a single matrix to enable comparison across samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/cross-sample-alignment-matrix-construction) · 2 copies)
- **cross-sample-cell-library-loss-quantification**: Use when multiplexed 10x Genomics droplet data sequenced on a patterned flow-cell instrument (HiSeq 4000/X/NovaSeq) needs per-sample assessment of how many cell calls are artefactual after swapped-molecule removal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-sample-cell-library-loss-quantification))
- **cross-sample-chip-signal-distribution-analysis**: Use when comparing ChIP-seq signal across ≥2 conditions with spike-in chromatin (e.g. drug titration series), especially when total-read normalization would mask a global occupancy reduction, and peak/background classification (MACS2 broad-peak BEDs) is available. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cross-sample-chip-signal-distribution-analysis))
